To address this, we introduce AGoQ, incorporating two new techniques: 1) a layer-aware activation quantization algorithm that allocates appropriate bit-widths for activations of various layers based on their types and pipeline stages to achieve near 4-bit activation storage, and 2) a gradient quantization algorithm that reduces memory usage and shortens communication time by employing 8-bit gradient storage and precision-preserving 8-bit All-Reduce communication. We conduct extensive experiments using different sizes of LLMs on two GPU clusters (up to 64 GPUs), and the experimental results show that our AGoQ reduces the memory by up to 52\% and achieves up to 1.34$\times$ improvement of training speed compared to state-of-the-art training systems Megatron-LM (w/ or w/o ZeRO), COAT and DeepSpeed with 8B to 32B LLaMA models, while achieving convergence loss on pretraining and comparable accuracy on downstream tasks with LLaMA architectures.

</details>


### [75] [A11y-Compressor: A Framework for Enhancing the Efficiency of GUI Agent Observations through Visual Context Reconstruction and Redundancy Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00551)
*Michito Takeshita,Takuro Kawada,Takumi Ohashi,Shunsuke Kitada,Hitoshi Iyatomi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出A11y-Compressor框架，将线性化无障碍树压缩为紧凑结构化表示，在OSWorld基准上将输入token降至22%，任务成功率平均提升5.1个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无障碍树（accessibility tree）作为GUI智能体的观察表示存在冗余且缺乏空间关系等结构信息，影响智能体对UI元素的可靠定位与理解。

Method: 提出A11y-Compressor框架，其实现Compressed-a11y包含模态检测、冗余缩减和语义结构化的轻量级结构化转换流水线，将线性化无障碍树转换为紧凑的结构化表示。

Result: 在OSWorld基准上，Compressed-a11y将输入token缩减至原始的22%，同时任务成功率平均提升5.1个百分点。

Conclusion: 压缩且结构化的无障碍树表示能有效减少冗余、保留关键信息，提升GUI智能体的任务执行效率与成功率。

Abstract: AI agents that interact with graphical user interfaces (GUIs) require effective observation representations for reliable grounding. The accessibility tree is a commonly used text-based format that encodes UI element attributes, but it suffers from redundancy and lacks structural information such as spatial relationships among elements. We propose A11y-Compressor, a framework that transforms linearized accessibility trees into compact and structured representations. Our implementation, Compressed-a11y, applies a lightweight and structured transformation pipeline with modal detection, redundancy reduction, and semantic structuring. Experiments on the OSWorld benchmark show that Compressed-a11y reduces input tokens to 22% of the original while improving task success rates by 5.1 percentage points on average.

</details>


### [76] [Beyond Decodability: Reconstructing Language Model Representations with an Encoding Probe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00607)
*Gaofei Shen,Martijn Bentum,Tom Lentz,Afra Alishahi,Grzegorz Chrupała*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种编码探针（Encoding Probe）方法，通过可解释特征重建模型内部表征，解决了传统解码探针无法直接比较特征贡献和受特征相关性影响的问题，并在文本和语音Transformer模型上验证了该方法能提供互补的解释视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统解码探针存在两个局限：不同特征对模型表征的贡献无法直接比较，以及特征相关性会影响探针结果。因此需要一种新的探针方法来克服这些问题。

Method: 提出编码探针（Encoding Probe），逆转传统解码探针的方向，使用可解释特征（涵盖声学、语音学、句法、词汇和说话人身份）重建模型的内部表征。

Result: 在文本和语音Transformer模型上的实验表明，说话人相关效应在不同训练目标和数据集间变化显著，而句法和词汇特征对重建的贡献是独立的。编码探针提供了超越可解码性的互补视角。

Conclusion: 编码探针是一种有效的补充性解释工具，能够揭示不同特征对模型表征的独立贡献，克服了传统解码探针的局限。

Abstract: Probing is widely used to study which features can be decoded from language model representations. However, the common decoding probe approach has two limitations that we aim to solve with our new encoding probe approach: contributions of different features to model representations cannot be directly compared, and feature correlations can affect probing results. We present an Encoding Probe that reverses this direction and reconstructs internal representations of models using interpretable features. We evaluate this method on text and speech transformer models, using feature sets spanning acoustics, phonetics, syntax, lexicon, and speaker identity. Our results suggest that speaker-related effects vary strongly across different training objectives and datasets, while syntactic and lexical features contribute independently to reconstruction. These results show that the Encoding Probe provides a complementary perspective on interpreting model representations beyond decodability.

</details>


### [77] [Is Textual Similarity Invariant under Machine Translation? Evidence Based on the Political Manifesto Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00618)
*Daria Boratyn,Damian Brzyski,Albert Leśniak,Wojciech Łukasik,Maciej Rapacz,Jan Rybicki,Wojciech Słomczyński,Dariusz Stolicki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了机器翻译下段落嵌入余弦相似度的不变性，利用2,800+份政党宣言的多语言语料库，通过成对相似度稳定性分析和跨模型校准，识别出10种语言具有翻译不变性、4种存在可检测失真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏系统框架来评估机器翻译对语义嵌入相似度关系的影响，尤其在多语言政治文本分析场景中，翻译是否保真地保留原始文本的语义结构尚不明确。

Method: 使用EU eTranslation服务将28种语言的2,800+政党宣言翻译为英语；不直接测量翻译引起的语义偏移，而是通过跨嵌入模型的成对相似度稳定性来评估；利用原始语言文本上的模型间不一致性作为校准的"不变性阈值"，构建每种语言的非劣效性检验框架。

Result: 在28种语言中，10种语言展现出翻译不变性（翻译后语义结构得到稳定保留），4种语言存在可检测的语义失真，其余语言证据不足以得出结论。

Conclusion: 该框架是语料库和流水线无关的，可自然扩展到下游任务，为评估机器翻译对语义嵌入影响提供了统计严谨的方法论。

Abstract: We investigate the extent to which cosine similarity between paragraph embeddings is invariant under machine translation, using the Manifesto Corpus of over 2,800 political party platforms in 28 languages translated to English via the EU eTranslation service. Rather than measuring translation-induced semantic shift directly we measure the stability of pairwise similarity relationships across embedding models, and use inter-model disagreement on original-language text as a calibrated invariance threshold. This yields a per-language non-inferiority test for four hypotheses about how translation interacts with embedding choice, with verdicts that distinguish languages where translation demonstrably preserves semantic structure from those where it demonstrably degrades it and from those where the available evidence does not resolve the question. The framework is corpus- and pipeline-agnostic and extends naturally to downstream tasks. Applied to our data, it identifies ten languages with translation invariance and four with detectable distortion.

</details>


### [78] [SC-Taxo: Hierarchical Taxonomy Generation under Semantic Consistency Constraints using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00620)
*Shiqiang Cai,Nianhong Niu,Shizhu He,Kang Liu,Jun Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SC-Taxo框架，利用大语言模型和层次感知精炼阶段生成语义一致的科技文献分类体系，通过双向标题生成机制（自底向上抽象+自顶向下约束）和同级语义依赖建模，解决现有方法的结构不一致和语义错位问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科技文献分类体系生成方法存在结构不一致和跨层级语义错位问题，根源在于对层级语义一致性的建模不足。

Method: 提出SC-Taxo框架，包含双向标题生成机制（自底向上抽象和自顶向下语义约束），并捕获同级语义依赖以增强横向一致性，利用大语言模型配合层次感知精炼阶段实现语义一致性。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上层次对齐和标题质量取得一致提升，在中文科技文献上验证了跨语言泛化能力。

Conclusion: SC-Taxo通过层次感知的双向生成和同级依赖建模，有效解决了分类体系生成中的语义一致性问题，并具有良好的跨语言泛化性。

Abstract: Scientific literature is expanding at an unprecedented pace, making it increasingly challenging to efficiently organize and access domain knowledge. A high-quality scientific taxonomy offers a structured and hierarchical representation of a research field, facilitating literature exploration and topic navigation, as well as enabling downstream applications such as trend analysis, idea generation, and information retrieval. However, existing taxonomy generation approaches often suffer from structural inconsistencies and semantic misalignment across hierarchical levels. Through empirical analysis, we find that these issues largely stem from inadequate modeling of hierarchical semantic consistency. To address this limitation, we propose a semantic-consistent taxonomy generation (SC-Taxo) framework that leverages large language models (LLMs) with hierarchy-aware refinement stages to ensure semantic consistency. Specifically, SC-Taxo introduces a bidirectional heading generation mechanism that jointly performs bottom-up abstraction and top-down semantic constraint, while further capturing peer-level semantic dependencies to enhance horizontal consistency. Experiments on multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate consistent improvements in hierarchy alignment and heading quality, and additional evaluation on Chinese scientific literature validates its robust cross-lingual generalization.

</details>


### [79] [Beyond Benchmarks: MathArena as an Evaluation Platform for Mathematics with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00674)
*Jasper Dekoninck,Nikola Jovanović,Tim Gehrunger,Kári Rögnvalddson,Ivo Petrov,Chenhao Sun,Martin Vechev*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MathArena从静态基准扩展为持续维护的数学推理评估平台，覆盖证明竞赛、研究级问题及Lean形式化证明，最强模型GPT-5.5在2026年USAMO上达98%，在研究级问题上达74%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态基准存在范围狭窄、易饱和、更新缓慢等问题，难以可靠比较模型性能并追踪进展，亟需持续维护的评估平台来全面衡量大模型在数学推理领域的能力。

Method: 在原有MathArena基础上大幅扩展评估范围，覆盖基于证明的竞赛题、研究级arXiv问题以及Lean形式化证明生成；保持清晰的评估协议，并随模型能力提升定期设计新基准以维持挑战性。

Result: GPT-5.5在2026年美国数学奥林匹克达到98%，在研究级数学问题上达到74%，表明前沿模型已能舒适解决极具挑战性的数学问题。

Conclusion: 持续维护的评估平台（如MathArena）对于追踪LLM在数学推理领域的快速进展至关重要。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are becoming increasingly capable mathematical collaborators, but static benchmarks are no longer sufficient for evaluating progress: they are often narrow in scope, quickly saturated, and rarely updated. This makes it hard to compare models reliably and track progress over time. Instead, we need evaluation platforms: continuously maintained systems that run, aggregate, and analyze evaluations across many benchmarks to give a comprehensive picture of model performance within a broad domain. In this work, we build on the original MathArena benchmark by substantially broadening its scope from final-answer olympiad problems to a continuously maintained evaluation platform for mathematical reasoning with LLMs. MathArena now covers a much wider range of tasks, including proof-based competitions, research-level arXiv problems, and formal proof generation in Lean. Additionally, we maintain a clear evaluation protocol for all models and regularly design new benchmarks as model capabilities improve to ensure that MathArena remains challenging. Notably, the strongest model, GPT-5.5, now reaches 98% on the 2026 USA Math Olympiad and 74% on research-level questions, showing that frontier models can now comfortably solve extremely challenging mathematical problems. This highlights the importance of continuously maintained evaluation platforms like MathArena to track the rapid progress of LLMs in mathematical reasoning.

</details>


### [80] [ML-Bench&Guard: Policy-Grounded Multilingual Safety Benchmark and Guardrail for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00689)
*Yunhan Zhao,Zhaorun Chen,Xingjun Ma,Yu-Gang Jiang,Bo Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了ML-Bench，一个基于区域法规构建的多语言安全基准（涵盖14种语言），以及ML-Guard，基于扩散大语言模型（dLLM）的护栏模型，支持多语言安全判断和基于政策条件的合规评估。实验表明ML-Guard在多项基准上持续优于11种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨语言安全基准大多依赖通用风险分类和机器翻译，无法与特定地区的法规和文化规范对齐，导致护栏模型难以适应不同司法管辖区的合规要求。

Method: 1) 直接从地区法规文本中提取风险类别和细粒度规则，引导生成多语言安全数据，构建ML-Bench（14种语言）；2) 基于扩散大语言模型（dLLM）构建ML-Guard，提供1.5B轻量版（快速安全/不安全判断）和7B增强版（自定义合规检查+详细解释）。

Result: 在6个现有跨语言安全基准和ML-Bench上，ML-Guard在11种强护栏基线方法中持续取得最优性能。

Conclusion: ML-Bench和ML-Guard有助于推动法规感知和文化对齐的多语言护栏系统的发展。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in cross-linguistic contexts, ensuring safety in diverse regulatory and cultural environments has become a critical challenge. However, existing multilingual benchmarks largely rely on general risk taxonomies and machine translation, which confines guardrail models to these predefined categories and hinders their ability to align with region-specific regulations and cultural nuances. To bridge these gaps, we introduce ML-Bench, a policy-grounded multilingual safety benchmark covering 14 languages. ML-Bench is constructed directly from regional regulations, where risk categories and fine-grained rules derived from jurisdiction-specific legal texts are directly used to guide the generation of multilingual safety data, enabling culturally and legally aligned evaluation across languages. Building on ML-Bench, we develop ML-Guard, a Diffusion Large Language Model (dLLM)-based guardrail model that supports multilingual safety judgment and policy-conditioned compliance assessment. ML-Guard has two variants, one 1.5B lightweight model for fast `safe/unsafe' checking and a more capable 7B model for customized compliance checking with detailed explanations. We conduct extensive experiments against 11 strong guardrail baselines across 6 existing multilingual safety benchmarks and our ML-Bench, and show that ML-Guard consistently outperforms prior methods. We hope that ML-Bench and ML-Guard can help advance the development of regulation-aware and culturally aligned multilingual guardrail systems.

</details>


### [81] [FinSafetyBench: Evaluating LLM Safety in Real-World Financial Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00706)
*Yutao Hou,Yihan Jiang,Yuhan Xie,Jian Yang,Liwen Zhang,Hailiang Huang,Guanhua Chen,Yun Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出FinSafetyBench，一个用于评估大语言模型在金融场景中合规安全性的双语红队基准测试集，涵盖14个金融犯罪与道德违规子类别。实验发现模型存在严重安全漏洞，中文语境下更易受攻击，且提示级防御难以抵御复杂隐性操纵策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在金融场景中可能产生有害输出（如协助非法活动或不道德行为），带来严重合规风险，而缺乏系统性评估金融领域LLM安全性的基准。

Method: 基于真实金融犯罪案例和伦理标准构建FinSafetyBench（中英双语），包含14个细分类别；在通用型和金融专用型LLM上设计三种代表性攻击设置进行实验。

Result: 发现对抗性提示可绕过合规防护的关键漏洞；中文语境下模型更易受攻击；提示级防御对复杂或隐性操纵策略效果有限。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在金融合规安全方面存在显著脆弱性，亟需更鲁棒的防御机制，尤其是在多语言和复杂攻击场景下。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied in financial scenarios. However, they may produce harmful outputs, including facilitating illegal activities or unethical behavior, posing serious compliance risks. To systematically evaluate LLM safety in finance, we propose FinSafetyBench, a bilingual (English-Chinese) red-teaming benchmark designed to test an LLM's refusal of requests that violate financial compliance. Grounded in real-world financial crime cases and ethics standards, the benchmark comprises 14 subcategories spanning financial crimes and ethical violations. Through extensive experiments on general-purpose and finance-specialized LLMs under three representative attack settings, we identify critical vulnerabilities that allow adversarial prompts to bypass compliance safeguards. Further analysis reveals stronger susceptibility in Chinese contexts and highlights the limitations of prompt-level defenses against sophisticated or implicit manipulation strategies.

</details>


### [82] [Characterizing the Expressivity of Local Attention in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00768)
*Jiaoda Li,Ryan Cotterell*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文从形式语言识别的角度解释了局部注意力机制为何能提升Transformer模型质量：局部注意力引入了第二个时序算子，严格扩展了可识别的正则语言类别，且全局与局部注意力在表达能力上互补，混合使用可获得最丰富的表达片段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部注意力虽能提升模型质量，但此前缺乏令人满意的理论解释。本文旨在从形式语言识别的角度提供这一现象的正式理论依据。

Method: 通过形式语言理论分析，证明固定精度Transformer与包含单一过去算子的线性时序逻辑片段对应；进一步证明添加局部注意力引入了第二个时序算子，严格扩展了可识别的正则语言类别。在形式语言识别和自然语言建模上进行实验验证。

Result: 全局注意力与局部注意力在表达能力上互补，互不包含；两者结合可获得最丰富的表达片段。实验表明混合全局-局部注意力Transformer在形式语言识别和自然语言建模上均优于纯全局注意力模型。

Conclusion: 局部注意力通过引入额外时序算子增强了Transformer的表达能力，全局与局部注意力具有表达互补性，混合使用是最优选择。

Abstract: The transformer is the most popular neural architecture for language modeling. The cornerstone of the transformer is its global attention mechanism, which lets the model aggregate information from all preceding tokens before generating the next token. One common variant of attention is called local attention, which restricts each token to aggregating information from a bounded window of predecessors, reducing the quadratic cost of global attention to linear. Although this restriction is usually motivated by efficiency, it has also been found to improve model quality, a phenomenon that has so far lacked a satisfactory explanation. We provide a formal account of this phenomenon in terms of recognizer expressivity. It has been shown that fixed-precision transformers with global attention correspond to a fragment of linear temporal logic containing a single past operator. We additionally prove that adding local attention introduces a second temporal operator, strictly enlarging the class of recognizable regular languages. Moreover, global and local attention are expressively complementary: neither subsumes the other, and combining them yields the richest fragment. Experiments on formal language recognition and natural language modeling corroborate the theory, showing that hybrid global--local transformers outperform their global-only counterparts.

</details>


### [83] [Directed Social Regard: Surfacing Targeted Advocacy, Opposition, Aid, Harms, and Victimization in Online Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00776)
*Scott Friedman,Ruta Wheelock,Sonja Schmer-Galunder,Drisana Iverson,Jake Vasilakes,Joan Zheng,Jeffrey Rye,Vasanth Sarathy,Christopher Miller*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出定向社会关注（DSR）方法，通过一对基于Transformer的模型实现多维度、多情感极性的细粒度情感分析，能够检测消息中跨度的情感目标并沿三条道德框架轴（-1,1）评分，在多个第三方数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NLP情感分析工具仅能对文本整体情感进行分类（正面、中性、负面），无法捕捉同一消息中正面与负面情感共存的现象，也无法报告这些情感的具体指向目标。在线平台语言、影响力操作和政治修辞中常混合亲社会与反社会情感，现有工具无法满足这一分析需求。

Method: 提出DSR方法，包含两个基于Transformer的模型：(1) 检测消息中情感目标的跨度级识别；(2) 沿三条由道德推脱和道德框架理论驱动的社会关注轴（-1,1）对跨度进行评分。同时介绍了DSR数据集构建的标注策略和验证研究。

Result: 在六个第三方在线媒体数据集上应用验证后的DSR模型，发现DSR输出与这些现有社会科学数据集中的标签和主题之间存在有意义的关联。

Conclusion: DSR方法有效实现了多情感极性、目标级细粒度情感分析，弥补了传统整体情感分类工具的不足，且在不同数据集上表现出良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: The language in online platforms, influence operations, and political rhetoric frequently directs a mix of pro-social sentiment (e.g., advocacy, helpfulness, compassion) and anti-social sentiment (e.g., threats, opposition, blame) at different topics, all in the same message. While many natural language processing (NLP) tools classify or score a text's overall sentiment as positive, neutral, or negative, these tools cannot report that positive and negative sentiments coexist, and they cannot report the target of those sentiments. This paper presents the Directed Social Regard (DSR) approach to multi-dimensional, multi-valence sentiment analysis, comprised of a pair of transformer-based models that (1) detects span-level targets of sentiment in a message and then (2) scores all spans within the message context along three (-1, 1) axes of regard that are motivated by social science theories of moral disengagement and moral framing. We present a data collection and annotation strategy for DSR dataset construction, a transformer-based architecture for span-level scoring, and a validation study with promising results. We apply the validated DSR model on six third-party datasets of online media and report meaningful correlations between DSR outputs and the labels and topics in these pre-existing social science datasets.

</details>


### [84] [When LLMs Stop Following Steps: A Diagnostic Study of Procedural Execution in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00817)
*Sailesh Panda,Pritam Kadasi,Abhishek Upperwal,Mayank Singh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过一个过程执行诊断基准测试，发现大语言模型在逐步算术算法上的首次回答准确率从5步程序的61%下降到95步程序的20%，暴露出模型在忠实执行指令方面的显著缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅凭最终答案准确率无法判断模型是否忠实地执行了提示中指定的过程，需要更细粒度的评估方法来揭示模型推理能力与指令执行忠实度之间的差距。

Method: 构建了一个受控的诊断基准，要求模型按照逐步算术算法和两个数值输入计算最终值，通过算法长度和中间变量的回溯依赖增加复杂度。在14个模型和55个数据集上进行测试，并进行生成级错误分析。

Result: 首次回答准确率从5步程序的61%降至95步程序的20%；模型失败模式包括：答案缺失、过早给出答案、初始错误后的自我修正、执行轨迹不完整以及幻觉式额外步骤。

Conclusion: 表面上的推理能力可能掩盖了忠实指令执行的重大缺陷，模型在过程执行方面存在系统性不足。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often achieve strong performance on reasoning benchmarks, but final-answer accuracy alone does not show whether they faithfully execute the procedure specified in a prompt. We study this question through a controlled diagnostic benchmark for procedural execution, where models are given a step-wise arithmetic algorithm and two numeric inputs, and must return the final computed value. The benchmark uses simple arithmetic operations but increases complexity through algorithm length and look-back dependencies over intermediate variables. Across 14 models and 55 datasets, average first-answer accuracy drops from 61% on 5-step procedures to 20% on 95-step procedures. Generation-level analysis shows that failures often involve missing answers, premature answers, self-correction after an initial error, under-executed traces, and hallucinated extra steps. These findings suggest that apparent reasoning ability can mask substantial weaknesses in faithful instruction execution.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [85] [A Survey of Reasoning-Intensive Retrieval: Progress and Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00063)
*Yiyang Wei,Tingyu Song,Siyue Zhang,Yilun Zhao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了推理密集型检索（RIR）领域，该领域关注查询与证据之间通过潜在推理关联而非语义相似性来中介相关性的检索场景。文章对现有基准、方法进行了分类，并提出了结构化分类体系，总结了挑战与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型推理能力的提升，研究者开始将这些能力整合到信息检索中，但该领域发展迅速且碎片化，缺乏系统性的框架来组织现有工作和明确前进方向。

Method: (1) 按知识领域和模态系统化梳理现有RIR基准；(2) 提出结构化分类法，根据推理在检索流程中的集成位置和方式对方法进行分类，并分析其权衡与实用场景；(3) 总结挑战与未来方向。

Result: 给出了RIR领域的系统性综述，包括基准分类、方法分类体系以及各方法的优缺点分析。

Conclusion: 该综述为RIR这一快速成长但碎片化的领域提供了清晰的路线图，有助于指导后续研究方向。

Abstract: Reasoning-Intensive Retrieval (RIR) targets retrieval settings where relevance is mediated by latent inferential links between a query and supporting evidence, rather than semantic similarity. Motivated by the emergent reasoning abilities of Large Language Models (LLMs), recent work integrates these capabilities into the IR field, spanning the entire pipeline from benchmarks to retrievers and rerankers. Despite this progress, the field lacks a systematic framework to organize current efforts and articulate a clear path forward. To provide a clear roadmap for this rapidly growing yet fragmented area, this survey (1) systematizes existing RIR benchmarks by knowledge domains and modalities, providing a detailed analysis of the current landscape; (2) introduces a structured taxonomy that categorizes methods based on where and how reasoning is integrated into the retrieval pipeline, alongside an analysis of their trade-offs and practical applications; and (3) summarizes challenges and future directions to guide research in this evolving field.

</details>


### [86] [Intelligent Elastic Feature Fading: Enabling Model Retrain-Free Feature Efficiency Rollouts at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00324)
*Jieming Di,Xiaoyu Chen,Ying She,Siyu Wang,Lizzie Liu,Fenggang Wu,Jiaoying Mu,Tony Tsui,Amr Elroumy,Hsing Tang,Zewei Jiang,Qiao Yang,Lin Qi,Haibo Lin,Weifeng Cui,Daniel Li,Kapil Gupta,Shivendra Pratap Singh,Jie Zheng,Arnold Overwijk,Ling Leng,Sri Reddy,Robert Malkin,Rocky Liu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出智能弹性特征衰减（IEFF）系统，通过服务时弹性控制特征覆盖率和分布，实现无需显式重训练的特征效率部署。该系统支持增量式特征覆盖调整，模型通过循环训练逐渐适应，包含严格安全护栏和可逆机制。在生产环境中，IEFF将效率相关部署加速5倍，消除重训练GPU开销，并实现更快容量回收。实验表明渐进式特征衰减相比突然移除可防止50-55%的在线性能退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模排序系统依赖跨多个时间窗口的用户行为特征，传统方法需要模型重训练来调整特征，导致迭代周期长（3-6个月）、GPU资源消耗大、部署吞吐量受限。

Method: 提出IEFF系统，在服务时弹性控制特征覆盖率和分布，支持增量式特征覆盖调整，通过循环训练让模型逐渐适应特征变化，无需显式重训练周期。系统包含安全护栏、可逆机制和全面监控。

Result: 效率部署加速5倍；消除重训练GPU开销；渐进式特征衰减防止50-55%的在线性能退化。

Conclusion: 弹性、系统级的特征衰减是管理现代工业排序系统中特征效率的实用且可扩展的方法。

Abstract: Large-scale ranking systems depend on thousands of features derived from user behavior across multiple time horizons. Typically requires model retraining -- resulting in long iteration cycles (3--6 months), substantial GPU resource consumption, and limited rollout throughput.
  We introduce Intelligent Elastic Feature Fading (IEFF), a production infrastructure system that enables retrain-free feature efficiency rollouts by elastically controlling feature coverage and distribution at serving time. IEFF supports incremental feature coverage adjustments while models adapt through recurring training, eliminating dependencies on explicit retraining cycles. The system incorporates strict safety guardrails, reversibility mechanisms, and comprehensive monitoring to ensure stability at scale.
  Across multiple production use cases, IEFF accelerates efficiency-related rollouts by 5$\times$, eliminates retraining-related GPU overhead, and enables faster capacity recycling. Extensive offline and online experiments demonstrate that gradual feature fading prevents 50--55\% of online performance degradation compared to abrupt feature removal, while maintaining stable model behavior. These results establish elastic, system-level feature fading as a practical and scalable approach for managing feature efficiency in modern industrial ranking systems.

</details>


### [87] [DynamicPO: Dynamic Preference Optimization for Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00327)
*Xingyu Hu,Kai Zhang,Jiancan Wu,Shuli Wang,Chi Wang,Wenshuai Chen,Yinhua Zhu,Haitao Wang,Xingxing Wang,Xiang Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文发现基于LLM的推荐系统中，直接偏好优化（DPO）在使用多负样本时会出现“偏好优化崩溃”现象（负样本增多导致性能下降），并理论证明该现象源于梯度抑制。为此提出DynamicPO框架，通过动态边界负样本选择和双边界动态beta调整两种自适应机制，有效防止优化崩溃并提升推荐精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于大语言模型的推荐系统中，DPO方法虽能有效对齐用户偏好，但增加负样本数量会导致性能退化（偏好优化崩溃），现有方法未能解释并解决这一反直觉现象。

Method: 提出DynamicPO框架，包含两个自适应机制：(1) 动态边界负样本选择，识别并优先选择靠近决策边界的信息性负样本；(2) 双边界动态beta调整，根据边界模糊度逐样本校准优化强度。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上，DynamicPO有效防止了优化崩溃，提升了多负样本偏好优化方法的推荐准确率，且计算开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: DynamicPO作为一种轻量级即插即用框架，通过缓解梯度抑制问题，有效解决了偏好优化崩溃，提升了LLM推荐系统的性能。

Abstract: In large language model (LLM)-based recommendation systems, direct preference optimization (DPO) effectively aligns recommendations with user preferences, requiring multi-negative objective functions to leverage abundant implicit-feedback negatives and sharpen preference boundaries. However, our empirical analyses reveal a counterintuitive phenomenon, preference optimization collapse, where increasing the number of negative samples can lead to performance degradation despite a continuously decreasing training loss. We further theoretically demonstrate that this collapse arises from gradient suppression, caused by the dominance of easily discriminable negatives over boundary-critical negatives that truly define user preference boundaries. As a result, boundary-relevant signals are under-optimized, weakening the model's decision boundary. Motivated by these observations, we propose DynamicPO (Dynamic Preference Optimization), a lightweight and plug-and-play framework comprising two adaptive mechanisms: Dynamic Boundary Negative Selection, which identifies and prioritizes informative negatives near the model's decision boundary, and Dual-Margin Dynamic beta Adjustment, which calibrates optimization strength per sample according to boundary ambiguity. Extensive experiments on three public datasets show that DynamicPO effectively prevents optimization collapse and improves recommendation accuracy on multi-negative preference optimization methods, with negligible computational overhead. Our code and datasets are available at https://github.com/xingyuHuxingyu/DynamicPO.

</details>


### [88] [Negative Data Mining for Contrastive Learning in Dense Retrieval at IKEA.com](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00353)
*Eva Agapaki,Amritpal Singh Gill*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出结构化负采样策略和基于LLM的评估方法，用于改进宜家产品搜索的稠密检索系统，在离线路测中取得了+2.6%的类别准确率提升，但在线A/B测试中长尾查询的用户参与度无显著差异，归因于高零点击率等用户搜索行为特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对比学习依赖负样本质量，而传统方法（随机采样、稀疏人工标注）难以生成语义上有挑战性的负样本，限制了稠密检索在宜家产品搜索中的效果。

Method: (1) 利用产品层次化分类体系和属性信息生成结构化的语义困难负样本；(2) 基于LLM作为评估器，对所有候选产品按查询进行评分，替代人工标注生成训练数据；构建于late-interaction检索架构之上。

Result: 加拿大市场真实用户查询的离线路测中，平均类别准确率提升+2.6%；在线长尾查询A/B测试中，用户参与度指标无统计学显著差异（p>0.05）。67%的热门搜索零点击率超过50%。

Conclusion: 困难负样本挖掘在离线检索质量上有效，但离线指标与在线用户参与度之间存在差距；必须将真实用户搜索行为（如查询意图分布和零点击模式）纳入训练数据和离线评估中才能弥合这一鸿沟。

Abstract: Contrastive learning is a core component of modern retrieval systems, but its effectiveness heavily relies on the quality of negative examples used during training. In this work, we present a systematic approach to improving dense retrieval for IKEA product search through structured negative sampling strategies and scalable LLM-as-a-judge relevance evaluation.
  Building on IKEA Search Engine's late-interaction retrieval architectures, we introduce two key contributions: (1) structured negative sampling strategies that leverage product hierarchical taxonomy and product attributes to generate semantically challenging negatives, and (2) a comprehensive LLM-based evaluation methodology for generating training data. Rather than relying on sparse human annotations or random sampling, our LLM-based evaluation system allocates a score for all candidate products against each query.
  Our methodology achieves +2.6\% average category accuracy on offline real user query experiments on the Canada market. However, our A/B test on long-tail queries showed no statistically significant differences in user engagement metrics between the improved and baseline models ($p > 0.05$). We trace this gap to user search behavior: 67\% of popular searches exhibit zero-click rates above 50\%, indicating that a substantial proportion of search sessions result in no product engagement regardless of result ranking. These findings underscore the importance of hard negative mining but also the need for grounding training data and offline evals in real user search behavior -- including query intent distribution and zero-click patterns -- to bridge the gap between offline retrieval quality and online user engagement.

</details>


### [89] [FollowTable: A Benchmark for Instruction-Following Table Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00400)
*Rihui Jin,Yuchen Lu,Ting Zhang,Jun Wang,Kuicai Dong,Zhaocheng Du,Dongping Liu,Gang Wang,Yong Liu,Guilin Qi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出指令遵循型表格检索（IFTR）任务，要求模型同时满足主题相关性及细粒度指令约束（如内容范围、模式约束）。构建了大规模基准FollowTable和新评估指标IRS，实验表明现有检索模型在该任务上表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统表格检索仅考虑主题语义相似性，但基于LLM的智能体系统对结构化数据的访问日益依赖指令驱动，相关性由显式内容和模式约束决定而非单纯主题相似性，因此需要形式化新任务。

Method: 形式化定义IFTR任务；通过分类驱动注释管道构建FollowTable基准；提出指令响应度得分（IRS）作为新评估指标。

Result: 现有检索模型难以遵循表格数据上的细粒度指令，表现出对表层语义线索的系统性偏置，且在处理模式约束方面能力有限。

Conclusion: 当前检索模型在处理IFTR任务上存在显著不足，未来有较大改进空间。

Abstract: Table Retrieval (TR) has traditionally been formulated as an ad-hoc retrieval problem, where relevance is primarily determined by topical semantic similarity. With the growing adoption of LLM-based agentic systems, access to structured data is increasingly instruction-driven, where relevance is conditional on explicit content and schema constraints rather than topical similarity alone. We therefore formalize Instruction-Following Table Retrieval (IFTR), a new task that requires models to jointly satisfy topical relevance and fine-grained instruction constraints. We identify two core challenges in IFTR: (i) sensitivity to content scope, such as inclusion and exclusion constraints, and (ii) awareness of schema-grounded requirements, including column semantics and representation granularity--capabilities largely absent in existing retrievers. To support systematic evaluation, we introduce FollowTable, the first large-scale benchmark for IFTR, constructed via a taxonomy-driven annotation pipeline. We further propose a new metric, termed the Instruction Responsiveness Score, to evaluate whether retrieval rankings consistently adapt to user instructions relative to a topic-only baseline. Our results indicate that existing retrieval models struggle to follow fine-grained instructions over tabular data. In particular, they exhibit systematic biases toward surface-level semantic cues and remain limited in handling schema-grounded constraints, highlighting substantial room for future improvements.

</details>


### [90] [Time-Interval-Aware Disentangled Expert Modeling for Next-Basket Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00499)
*Zhiying Deng,Yuan Fu,Usman Farooq,Ziwei Tian,Wei Liu,Jianjun Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出TIDE模型，通过解耦习惯性回购和探索性兴趣两种用户意图，并结合时间间隔建模，显著提升了下一次购物篮推荐（NBR）的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NBR方法将习惯性回购和探索性兴趣两种冲突意图纠缠在单一表示中，导致习惯掩盖探索；同时依赖离散序列建模，忽略了连续时间间隔和物品特定周期性，限制了推荐性能。

Method: TIDE包含三个核心组件：(1) Hawkes-enhanced Fourier Time Encoding，捕捉物品特定的时间周期性和动态衰减；(2) 双专家架构，包括Habit Expert（处理重复需求）和Pattern-Guided Exploration Expert（处理探索发现）；(3) 物品感知的门控机制，自适应平衡回购与探索。

Result: 在四个真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，TIDE consistently优于代表性的最先进NBR方法。

Conclusion: 通过解耦用户意图和引入时间间隔建模，TIDE有效解决了NBR中习惯与探索的冲突问题，为购物篮推荐提供了更准确的预测方案。

Abstract: Next-basket recommendation (NBR) is a type of recommendation that aims to predict a set of items a user will purchase based on their historical transaction basket sequences. It is governed by a dynamic interplay between two distinct user intents: habitual repurchase, which involves repeating past behaviors, and exploratory interest, which involves discovering new items. However, existing NBR methods generally suffer from two limitations: (1) they often entangle these conflicting motives within a single representation, causing habits to overshadow discovery, and (2) they rely on discrete sequential modeling that ignores continuous-time intervals and item-specific periodicities. In this paper, we propose a novel solution named Time-Interval Disentangled Experts (TIDE) to address these challenges. TIDE incorporates a Hawkes-enhanced Fourier Time Encoding to capture item-specific temporal periodicities and dynamic decay. To decouple user intentions, TIDE utilizes a dual-expert architecture that integrates a Habit Expert for recurring needs and a Pattern-Guided Exploration Expert for discovery. Combined with an item-aware gating mechanism, TIDE adaptively balances repurchase and exploration. Extensive experiments on four diverse real-world datasets demonstrate that TIDE consistently outperforms representative state-of-the-art NBR methods.

</details>


### [91] [LLM-Oriented Information Retrieval: A Denoising-First Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00505)
*Lu Dai,Liang Sun,Fanpu Cao,Ziyang Rao,Cehao Yang,Hao Liu,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出信息检索正从面向人类转向面向大语言模型（LLM），LLM受限于有限的注意力预算且对噪声敏感，因此去噪——最大化上下文窗口中的可验证证据密度——成为整个信息访问管线的核心瓶颈。文章通过四阶段框架（不可访问→不可发现→不对齐→不可验证）阐释这一范式转变，并提供了涵盖索引、检索、上下文工程、验证和智能体工作流的信号-噪声优化技术分类体系，最后在终身助手、编码智能体、深度研究和多模态理解等检索密集型领域展示了去噪研究成果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代信息检索的主要消费者已从人类转变为通过RAG和智能体搜索使用检索结果的大语言模型，而LLM与人类不同，其注意力预算有限且对噪声极为敏感——误导性或无关信息不再是简单干扰，而是直接导致幻觉和推理失败。因此，需要重新思考IR在LLM时代的核心挑战与优化方向。

Method: 采用观点论文（perspective paper）形式，构建了一个四阶段框架（不可访问→不可发现→不对齐→不可验证）来描述IR挑战的范式转变，并提供了按管线组织的信号-噪声优化技术分类法，涵盖索引、检索、上下文工程、验证和智能体工作流五个环节。

Result: 识别出去噪——在上下文窗口内最大化可用的证据密度和可验证性——成为整个信息访问管线的核心瓶颈；展示了跨索引、检索、上下文工程、验证和智能体工作流五个环节的技术分类体系；在终身助手、编码智能体、深度研究和多模态理解等检索密集型领域验证了信息去噪的研究工作。

Conclusion: 随着LLM成为信息检索的主要消费者，IR的核心目标应从"提供相关信息"转向"提供可验证的去噪信息"，需要系统性地在整个管线中优化信号-噪声比，以减轻LLM的幻觉和推理失败问题。

Abstract: Modern information retrieval (IR) is no longer consumed primarily by humans but increasingly by large language models (LLMs) via retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) and agentic search. Unlike human users, LLMs are constrained by limited attention budgets and are uniquely vulnerable to noise; misleading or irrelevant information is no longer just a nuisance, but a direct cause of hallucinations and reasoning failures. In this perspective paper, we argue that denoising-maximizing usable evidence density and verifiability within a context window-is becoming the primary bottleneck across the full information access pipeline. We conceptualize this paradigm shift through a four-stage framework of IR challenges: from inaccessible to undiscoverable, to misaligned, and finally to unverifiable. Furthermore, we provide a pipeline-organized taxonomy of signal-to-noise optimization techniques, spanning indexing, retrieval, context engineering, verification, and agentic workflow. We also present research works on information denoising in domains that rely heavily on retrieval such as lifelong assistant, coding agent, deep research, and multimodal understanding.

</details>


### [92] [MUDY: Multi-Granular Dynamic Candidate Contextualization for Unsupervised Keyphrase Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00597)
*Hyeongu Kang,Susik Yoon*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出MUDY框架，通过提示评分和自注意力评分两种互补组件，捕捉候选关键词的多粒度上下文显著性，在四个真实数据集上超越了现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于预训练语言模型的关键词提取方法虽能捕捉全局语义相关性，但在捕捉与文档中分散的特定子话题相关的局部上下文重要性方面存在不足。

Method: MUDY框架包含两个互补组件：(1) 基于提示的评分，估计每个候选关键词的生成可能性，并通过候选感知加权反映局部上下文重要性；(2) 基于自注意力的评分，利用PLM的多粒度注意力模式，在文档级和片段级评估候选词显著性。

Result: 在四个真实数据集上，MUDY在不同截断阈值下的top-k准确率均优于最先进的基线方法。定量和定性分析进一步验证了基于多粒度显著性的上下文中心关键词提取的有效性。

Conclusion: MUDY通过多粒度上下文显著性捕捉，有效提升了关键词提取性能，代码已开源以供复现。

Abstract: Keyphrase extraction aims to automatically identify concise phrases that effectively represent the content of a document. While recent methods leveraging pre-trained language models (PLMs) have significantly improved the extraction of keyphrases with strong global semantic relevance, they often fall short in capturing the local contextual importance of keyphrases tied to specific subtopics dispersed in a document. In this paper, we propose a novel context-centric framework, MUDY, that effectively captures multi-granular contextual salience of candidate keyphrases. MUDY employs two complementary components: (1) a prompt-based scoring that estimates the generation likelihood of each candidate keyphrase, augmented with candidate-aware weighting to better reflect its local contextual importance, and (2) a self-attention-based scoring that utilizes multi-granular attention patterns from PLMs to assess candidate significance at both the document-wide and segment-specific levels. Evaluations on four real-world datasets demonstrate that MUDY outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in top-k accuracy at various cutoff thresholds. In-depth quantitative and qualitative analyses further highlight the efficacy of context-centric keyphrase extraction with multi-granular saliency. For reproducibility, the source code of MUDY is available at https://github.com/HgKang1/MUDY.

</details>


### [93] [A Replicability Study of XTR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00646)
*Rohan Jha,Reno Kriz,Benjamin Van Durme*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文复现并扩展了XTR算法，发现其训练修改虽未在整体检索效果上优于ColBERT，但能扁平化token得分分布、提升IVF引擎效率，从而在基于IVF的部署环境中具有实用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证XTR算法是否真的优于ColBERT，并探究其训练修改在不同检索引擎和训练范式下的实际效果与机制。

Method: 复现XTR的检索算法和改进的训练目标，扩展评估到知识蒸馏训练以及PLAID和WARP两种高效检索引擎。

Result: 确认了XTR的token级匹配特性，但未能复现其在受控比较下优于ColBERT的整体效果；不过发现XTR训练能扁平化ColBERT的token得分分布，从而产生更具区分性的质心分数，提升IVF检索效率。

Conclusion: XTR训练的实际价值不限于原始研究中的低k'场景，而适用于任何使用IVF引擎的部署场景，为从业者提供了何时以及如何使用XTR的具体指导。

Abstract: The XTR (conteXtual Token Retrieval) algorithm is a modification to ColBERT retrieval that avoids the costly step of fully gathering and reranking the candidates' embeddings by imputing their missing similarity scores from the initial token retrieval step. The original work proposes a modified training objective as necessary for effective XTR retrieval, arguing that standard ColBERT token scoring is unsuitable for imputation. In this paper, we replicate both the XTR retrieval algorithm and its modified training objective, and extend the evaluation to knowledge-distillation (KD) training and efficient retrieval engines (PLAID and WARP). We confirm the token-level matching characteristics claimed in the original work, but fail to replicate XTR's overall effectiveness advantage over ColBERT under a controlled comparison. We further show that XTR's training modification has a concrete mechanistic consequence for modern retrieval engines: by flattening ColBERT's characteristically peaked token score distribution, XTR training yields more discriminative centroid scores and thus more efficient IVF-based retrieval under PLAID and WARP. The utility of XTR training is therefore not limited to the low-$k'$ regime originally studied, but extends to any deployment setting where IVF-based engines are used. These findings offer practitioners concrete guidance on how and when to use XTR as their multi-vector retriever.

</details>


### [94] [Robust Multimodal Recommendation via Graph Retrieval-Enhanced Modality Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00670)
*Yuan Li,Jun Hu,Jiaxin Jiang,Bryan Hooi,Bingsheng He*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出GRE-MC框架，通过图检索增强机制为缺失模态的节点从全图中检索语义相关子图，并利用图Transformer联合编码查询节点与检索子图完成模态补全，在推荐任务上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态推荐系统中真实数据常因传感器故障、标注稀缺或隐私约束导致模态不完整，现有补全方法仅依赖节点自身或邻居信息，忽略图中语义相关的上下文线索。

Method: 提出GRE-MC框架：1) 模态感知子图检索机制从全图中选取语义相关子图；2) 图Transformer通过全局注意力联合编码查询节点与检索子图；3) 可学习稀疏路由码本将潜在嵌入正则化为紧凑基向量以提升鲁棒性。

Result: 在多模态推荐基准上，GRE-MC一致优于当前最先进方法，验证了子图检索与联合编码图Transformer对鲁棒模态补全的有效性。

Conclusion: 图检索增强的模态补全能够有效利用全局语义上下文，显著提升多模态不完整场景下的推荐性能。

Abstract: Multimodal data plays a critical role in web-based recommendation systems, where information from diverse modalities such as vision and text enhances representation learning. However, real-world multimodal datasets often suffer from modality incompleteness due to sensor failures, annotation scarcity, or privacy constraints, which substantially degrade model performance and reliability. One effective solution to address this issue is modality completion, which reconstructs missing features to provide modality-complete graphs for downstream tasks. Given a query node with missing multimodal features, existing modality completion methods typically infer information from the node itself or its neighbors to reconstruct the missing modality. However, these methods may overlook semantically relevant context in the graph, which contains valuable cues that are non-trivial to capture through simple methods like neighborhood aggregation. In this work, we propose GRE-MC, a Graph Retrieval-Enhanced Modality Completion framework, to overcome these limitations. By introducing a modality-aware subgraph retrieval mechanism, GRE-MC selects semantically relevant subgraphs from the entire graph, providing richer contextual information for completing missing modalities. Subsequently, a graph transformer jointly encodes the query node and the retrieved subgraph via global attention to complete the missing features, while a learnable sparse-routing codebook regularizes latent embeddings into compact bases for improved robustness. Extensive experiments on multimodal recommendation benchmarks demonstrate that GRE-MC consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, validating the effectiveness of subgraph retrieval and joint-encoding graph transformer for robust modality completion.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [95] [FedACT: Concurrent Federated Intelligence across Heterogeneous Data Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00011)
*Md Sirajul Islam,Isabelle G Chapman,N I Md Ashafuddula,Xu Yuan,Li Chen,Nian-Feng Tzeng,Klara Nahrstedt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedACT 是一种面向多联邦学习任务并发场景的资源异构感知设备调度框架，通过对齐评分机制和参与公平性策略，最小化平均任务完成时间并提升模型精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习研究大多聚焦于单任务优化，而真实场景需要多个FL任务同时在共享设备池上训练。直接应用单任务优化技术会导致次优性能，尤其是设备异构性和资源效率低下的问题。

Method: 提出 FedACT 框架，核心包括：(1) 对齐评分机制，评估设备可用资源与任务资源需求的匹配度；(2) 参与公平性策略，确保设备在各任务间均衡贡献；(3) 基于对齐分数优先级的优化调度方案，最小化平均作业完成时间。

Result: 在多种FL任务和基准数据集上的实验表明，与现有最优基线相比，FedACT 将平均作业完成时间降低至原来的 1/8.3（最多8.3倍加速），模型精度提升高达 44.5%。

Conclusion: FedACT 有效解决了多FL并发场景下的设备调度问题，兼顾了异构资源适配和参与公平性，显著提升了系统效率和模型质量。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables collaborative intelligence across decentralized data source devices in a privacy-preserving way. While substantial research attention has been drawn to optimizing the learning process for an individual task, real-world applications increasingly require multiple machine learning tasks simultaneously training their models across a shared pool of devices. Naively applying single-FL optimization techniques in multi-FL systems results in suboptimal system performance, particularly due to device heterogeneity and resource inefficiency. To address such a critical open challenge, we introduce {\em FedACT}, a novel resource heterogeneity-aware device scheduling approach designed to efficiently schedule heterogeneous devices across multiple concurrent FL jobs, with the goal of minimizing their average job completion time (JCT). {\em FedACT} dynamically assigns devices to FL jobs based on an alignment scoring mechanism that evaluates the compatibility between available resources of devices and resource demands of jobs. Additionally, it incorporates participation fairness to ensure balanced contributions from devices across jobs, further enhancing the accuracy levels of learned global models. An optimal scheduling plan is formulated in {\em FedACT} by prioritizing devices with higher alignment scores, while ensuring fair participation across jobs. To evaluate the effectiveness of the proposed scheduling algorithm, we carried out comprehensive experiments using diverse FL jobs and benchmark datasets. Experimental results demonstrate that {\em FedACT} reduces the average JCT by up to 8.3\(\times\) and improves model accuracy by up to 44.5\%, compared to the state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [96] [What Physics do Data-Driven MoCap-to-Radar Models Learn?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00018)
*Kevin Chen,Kenneth W. Parker,Anish Arora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于物理的可解释性框架，通过两个互补指标（多普勒频率对齐度、速度干预下的频率关系保持度）评估数据驱动的MoCap-to雷达模型是否真正学习了物理规律。实验表明低重建误差不保证物理一致性，部分模型虽误差低但物理指标差，且时间注意力对Transformer模型学习物理规律至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究数据驱动的MoCap-to雷达模型是否真正学习了底层物理规律，而不仅仅是拟合数据分布。

Method: 提出基于物理的可解释性框架，包含两个互补指标：1) 模型预测与物理推导的多普勒频率对齐度；2) 速度干预下预测是否保持速度-频率关系。两个指标仅需MoCap输入和模型预测，无需实测雷达数据。

Result: 低重建误差不保证物理一致性；部分模型误差低但物理指标表现差；时间注意力对Transformer模型学习物理规律至关重要。

Conclusion: 现有数据驱动模型存在"拟合数据而非学习物理"的问题，物理可解释性指标比单纯的重建误差更能反映模型对物理规律的理解程度。

Abstract: Data-driven MoCap-to-radar models generate plausible micro-Doppler spectrograms, but do they actually learn the underlying physics? We introduce a physics-based interpretability framework to answer this question via two proposed complementary metrics: one measures alignment between model predictions and the physics-derived Doppler frequency, while the other tests whether predictions preserve the velocity-frequency relationship under velocity intervention. Both metrics require only MoCap input and model predictions, without access to measured radar data. Experiments across several model architectures reveal that low reconstruction error does not guarantee physical consistency: some, but not all, models achieve low error yet perform poorly on the two physics-based metrics. Further analysis shows that temporal attention is critical for transformer-based models to learn the underlying physics.

</details>


### [97] [Learning physically grounded traffic accident reconstruction from public accident reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00050)
*Yanchen Guan,Haicheng Liao,Chengyue Wang,Zhenning Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于公开事故报告和场景测量的参数化多模态学习方法，构建了包含6,217个真实事故案例的CISS-REC数据集，并开发了从文本语义到车道一致运动重建及碰撞交互优化的重建框架，在多项指标上优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通事故报告普遍存在，但基于这些文本进行物理上准确的事故重建却因详细场景测量和专家重建数据的稀缺、昂贵且难以规模化而面临困难。

Method: 构建CISS-REC数据集（6,217个真实事故案例），开发多模态重建框架：将报告语义与道路拓扑和参与者属性对齐，重建车道一致的事前运动轨迹，通过局部几何推理和时间分配优化碰撞相关交互。

Result: 在CISS-REC数据集上优于代表性基线方法，取得了最强的整体重建保真度，包括改进的事故点精度和碰撞一致性。

Conclusion: 公开事故报告可作为可扩展的计算基底用于定量可验证的事故重建，对交通安全分析、仿真和自动驾驶研究具有潜在价值。

Abstract: Traffic accidents are routinely documented in textual reports, yet physically grounded accident reconstruction remains difficult because detailed scene measurements and expert reconstructions are scarce, costly and hard to scale. Here we formulate accident reconstruction from publicly accessible reports and scene measurements as a parameterized multimodal learning problem. We construct CISS-REC, a dataset of 6,217 real-world accident cases curated from the NHTSA Crash Investigation Sampling System, and develop a reconstruction framework that grounds report semantics to road topology and participant attributes, reconstructs lane consistent pre-impact motion, and refines collision relevant interactions through localized geometric reasoning and temporal allocation. Our method outperforms representative baselines on CISS-REC, achieving the strongest overall reconstruction fidelity, including improved accident point accuracy and collision consistency. These results show that public accident reports can serve as scalable computational substrates for quantitatively verifiable accident reconstruction, with potential value for traffic safety analysis, simulation and autonomous driving research.

</details>


### [98] [Smart Ensemble Learning Framework for Predicting Groundwater Heavy Metal Pollution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00056)
*T. Ansah-Narh,G. Y. Afrifa,J. B. Tandoh,K. Asare,M. Addi,K. E. Yorke,D. M. A. Akpoley,K. Aidoo,S. K. Fosuhene*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究针对Densu盆地地下水重金属污染指数（HPI）的统计复杂性和空间异质性，构建了集成响应变换与嵌套交叉验证的集成机器学习预测框架。比较了原始尺度、对数变换和高斯Copula三种变换策略在六种学习器上的表现。结果表明，原始尺度模型存在过度乐观（R²≈1.0），对数变换稳定了方差（SVM: R²=0.93），高斯Copula方法最为可靠（集成模型R²=0.96，RMSE=0.19），并结合DBSCAN聚类识别出Fe和Mn为主要贡献因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Densu盆地地下水重金属污染日益严重，传统方法无法捕捉污染指标的统计复杂性和空间异质性。HPI通常呈偏态分布且受相关污染物影响，不经变换直接建模会导致预测偏差。

Method: 提出集成响应变换与嵌套交叉验证集成机器学习的预测框架：对HPI采用三种变换策略（原始、对数、高斯Copula），评估六种学习器（SVM、k-NN、CART、Elastic Net、核岭回归、堆叠Lasso集成），并结合DBSCAN聚类进行贡献因子分析。

Result: 原始尺度模型R²≈1.0存在过度乐观；对数变换SVM R²=0.93、RMSE=0.18，k-NN R²=0.92、RMSE=0.20；高斯Copula集成模型R²=0.96、RMSE=0.19。DBSCAN聚类识别Fe和Mn为主要贡献因子。

Conclusion: 分布感知的集成学习结合聚类诊断能够为地下水污染评估提供稳健且可解释的方案。局限在于使用随机交叉验证而非空间交叉验证，且仅针对单一盆地。

Abstract: Groundwater in the Densu Basin is increasingly threatened by heavy metal contamination, but conventional methods fail to capture the statistical complexity and spatial heterogeneity of pollution indicators. A key challenge is modelling the Heavy Metal Pollution Index (HPI), which is typically skewed and affected by correlated contaminants, leading to biased predictions without transformation. This study develops a predictive framework integrating response transformations with nested cross-validated ensemble machine learning. Three transformations (raw, log, and Gaussian copula) were applied to HPI and evaluated across six learners: support vector regression (SVM), $k$-nearest neighbours (k-NN), CART, Elastic Net, kernel ridge regression, and a stacked Lasso ensemble. Raw-scale models produced deceptively high fits (Elastic Net and stacked ensemble $R^2 \approx 1.0$), suggesting over-optimism. The log transformation stabilised variance (SVM: $R^2 = 0.93$, RMSE $= 0.18$; k-NN: $R^2 = 0.92$, RMSE $= 0.20$). The Gaussian copula gave the most reliable results: stacked ensemble $R^2 = 0.96$ (RMSE $= 0.19$), with other learners maintaining high accuracy. Copula-based models improved residuals and produced spatially plausible maps. DBSCAN clustering revealed Fe and Mn as primary HPI contributors, consistent with regional hydrogeochemistry. Limitations include reliance on random (not spatial) cross-validation and basin-specific scope. Future work should explore spatial validation and other geological settings. Overall, distribution-aware ensembles with clustering diagnostics offer robust, interpretable assessments of groundwater contamination.

</details>


### [99] [Information-Theoretic Generalization Bounds for Stochastic Gradient Descent with Predictable Virtual Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00064)
*Mohammad Partohaghighi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可预测的历史自适应虚拟扰动方法，使SGD的泛化界能够适应优化路径中的几何信息（如梯度统计、预条件子等），通过条件高斯相对熵论证，在保持算法不变的前提下扩展了虚拟扰动分析到历史依赖的SGD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的信息论泛化界中，虚拟扰动协方差需固定且独立于优化历史，无法表征由动态梯度统计、预条件子等路径信息诱导的几何结构，限制了泛化界的表达能力和适用性。

Method: 引入可预测的历史自适应虚拟扰动：每次迭代的扰动协方差可依赖过去真实SGD历史（而非当前或未来随机性），通过条件高斯相对熵论证导出泛化界；将全局参考核比较与局部高斯平滑分离，引入协方差比较代价项。

Result: 得到了具有条件自适应灵敏度项和偏差项的新泛化界；在可容许同步（确定性、公开或前缀可观测协方差规则）下可恢复固定噪声风格的界；统一了固定各向同性和几何感知界作为特例。

Conclusion: 该方法在不修改SGD算法本身的前提下，将虚拟扰动分析从固定噪声扩展到了历史依赖的自适应噪声几何，显著增强了信息论泛化界对自适应优化过程的刻画能力。

Abstract: Information-theoretic generalization bounds analyze stochastic optimization by relating expected generalization error to the mutual information between learned parameters and training data. Virtual perturbation analyses of SGD add auxiliary Gaussian noise only in the proof, making mutual information tractable while leaving the actual SGD trajectory unchanged. Existing bounds, however, typically require perturbation covariances to be fixed independently of the optimization history, limiting their ability to represent geometries induced by moving gradient statistics, preconditioners, curvature proxies, and other pathwise information.
  We introduce predictable history-adaptive virtual perturbations, where the perturbation covariance at each iteration may depend on the past real SGD history but not on current or future randomness. This predictability enables a conditional Gaussian relative-entropy argument and yields generalization bounds for SGD with adaptive virtual-noise geometry. The bounds replace fixed sensitivity and gradient-deviation terms with conditional adaptive counterparts, include an output-sensitivity penalty from accumulated perturbation covariance, and reduce the deviation term to a conditional variance only under conditional unbiasedness.
  Since adaptive covariances may be data-dependent, we separate local Gaussian smoothing from global reference-kernel comparison. The resulting bound includes a covariance-comparison cost measuring the KL price of using an admissible reference geometry different from the actual adaptive covariance. Fixed-noise-style bounds are recovered under admissible synchronization, such as deterministic, public, or prefix-observable covariance rules. The framework recovers fixed isotropic and geometry-aware bounds as special cases while extending virtual perturbation analysis to history-dependent SGD without modifying the algorithm.

</details>


### [100] [CRADIPOR: Crash Dispersion Predictor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00070)
*Edgar Chaillou,Sebastian Rodriguez,Yves Tourbier,Francisco Chinesta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CRADIPOR工具，利用秩降自编码器（RRAE）结合监督分类，在无需重复计算的情况下预测汽车碰撞仿真中的数值离散性，实验表明该方法优于随机森林基线，其中基于斜率的信号表征分类性能最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 汽车碰撞有限元仿真因并行计算和模型复杂性导致结果不可严格重复，后处理中性能指标存在显著数值离散性，影响工程决策。重复仿真估算离散性计算成本过高，因此需要一种无需重复计算即可预测离散性的工具。

Method: 采用秩降自编码器（RRAE）结合监督分类，在常规碰撞仿真后处理中识别对数值离散性敏感的区域，并与随机森林基线进行对比，测试了多种信号表征（如小波基、斜率变化等）。

Result: RRAE框架在数据集上优于随机森林基线；小波基和基于斜率的输入信号表现最佳，其中斜率变化的分类性能最优。

Conclusion: 结构化潜在表征有助于提升汽车碰撞后处理中数值离散性的检测效果，RRAE框架可用于实际工程中的离散性预测。

Abstract: We present CRADIPOR, a numerical dispersion prediction tool for automotive crash simulations. Finite Element (FE) crash models are widely used throughout vehicle development, but their predictions are not strictly repeatable because of parallel computation and model complexity. As a result, performance criteria evaluated during post-processing may exhibit significant numerical dispersion, which complicates engineering decision-making. Although dispersion can be estimated by repeating the same simulation, this approach is generally impractical because of its high computational cost.
  This work therefore investigates a prediction tool that can be applied during routine crash-simulation post-processing without repeating the computation. The proposed approach relies on a Rank Reduction Autoencoder (RRAE) combined with supervised classification in order to identify regions sensitive to numerical dispersion. The comparative analysis suggests that the RRAE-based framework is more effective than the Random Forest baseline on the studied dataset. Among the tested signal representations, wavelet-based and slope-based inputs appear to be the most promising, with slope variations providing the best classification performance. These results support the use of structured latent representations for improving numerical-dispersion detection in automotive crash post-processing.

</details>


### [101] [Hyperspherical Forward-Forward with Prototypical Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00082)
*Shalini Sarode,Brian Moser,Joachim Folz,Federico Raue,Tobias Nauen,Stanislav Frolov,Andreas Dengel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出超球面前向-前向（HFF）算法，将原始FF算法的二值适配度任务重构为超球面特征空间中的直接多类分类问题，通过学习类别特定的单位范数原型作为几何锚点和隐式负样本，实现了单次前向传播完成权重更新和推理，速度比原始FF算法快40倍以上，并在ImageNet-1k上首次突破25% top-1准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始前向-前向（FF）算法虽然训练高效，但推理过程计算开销极大，需要对每个类别单独进行一次前向传播，严重限制了其实用性。

Method: 将每层的局部目标从二值适配度任务重构为超球面特征空间中的多类分类问题，学习一组类别特定的单位范数原型作为几何锚点和隐式负样本，使得权重更新和推理可在单次前向传播中完成。

Result: 推理速度比原始FF算法快40倍以上；在ImageNet-1k上首次实现超过25%的top-1准确率（作为首批贪心局部学习方法之一），迁移学习达到65.96%准确率。

Conclusion: HFF保留了局部训练的优势，同时消除了FF算法的推理瓶颈，有效缩小了与反向传播的性能差距，且易于实现、可扩展至现代卷积架构。

Abstract: The Forward-Forward (FF) algorithm presents a compelling, bio-inspired alternative to backpropagation. However, while efficient in training, it has a computationally prohibitive inference process that requires a separate forward pass for every class that is evaluated. In this work, we introduce the Hyperspherical Forward-Forward (HFF), a novel reformulation that resolves this critical bottleneck. Our core innovation is to reframe the local objective of each layer from a binary goodness-of-fit task to a direct multi-class classification problem within a hyperspherical feature space. We achieve this by learning a set of class-specific, unit-norm prototypes that act as geometric anchors and implicit negatives. This architectural innovation preserves the benefits of local training while enabling weight update and inference in a single forward pass, making it >40x faster than the original FF algorithm. Our method is simple to implement, scales effectively to modern convolutional architectures, and achieves superior accuracy on standard image classification benchmarks, closing the gap with backpropagation. Most notably, we are among the first greedy local-learning methods to report over 25% top-1 accuracy on ImageNet-1k, and 65.96% with transfer learning.

</details>


### [102] [Comparative Analysis of Polygon-Based and Global Machine Learning Models for Bus Occupancy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00083)
*Daniel Azenkot,Michael Fire,Eran Ben Elia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过空间聚类将城市划分为不同区域，并为每个区域训练独立预测模型的公交通勤量预测框架，在保持与全局模型相当精度的同时实现了对局部动态的有效建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统预测模型将整个城市视为同质区域，无法捕捉不同城区的独特和局部化动态，导致预测精度受限。

Method: 利用空间聚类方法（基于邻近公交站共享相似客流量特征的原理）将城市划分为不同区域，结合多维特征数据（公交客流量、空间特征、气象条件、时间模式），为每个聚类区域训练独立的局部预测模型。

Result: 局部化预测模型达到了与全局模型相当的预测精度。

Conclusion: 具有空间感知的局部化建模策略对公共交通预测是有效的，为实现更有针对性和高效的服务改进铺平了道路。

Abstract: Accurate forecasting of bus ridership (passengers numbers) is crucial for efficient management and optimization of public transport systems. Traditional forecasting models often fail to capture the unique and localized dynamics of different urban areas by treating the entire city as a single, homogeneous region. This paper introduces a novel framework that enhances bus ridership prediction by integrating a spatial clustering methodology with multi-dimensional feature analysis. The proposed framework utilizes a diverse set of data, including bus ridership data (by route number, time, and bus stop) complemented by a variety of open source data, such as spatial features (e.g., attractive destinations), meteorological conditions (e.g., temperature, rainfall), and temporal patterns (e.g., time of day, day of week). By clustering the urban area into distinct regions, based on the principle that bus stops in close proximity share similar ridership characteristics, a separate local forecasting model is trained for each of these clusters. This localized approach demonstrates an accuracy comparable to that of global models. The findings suggest that a spatially-aware, localized modeling strategy is effective for public transport prediction, paving the way for more targeted and efficient service improvements.

</details>


### [103] [SPLICE: Latent Diffusion over JEPA Embeddings for Conformal Time-Series Inpainting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00126)
*Arnaud Zinflou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SPLICE框架，将潜变量生成式插补与自适应共形预测相结合，为电力系统时间序列插补提供具有有限样本可靠性保证的解决方案。在十三个负荷数据集上，SPLICE实现了最优的MSE（0.056）和CRPS（0.161），ACI模块提供93-95%的经验覆盖，并展现出跨域迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式时间序列插补模型虽重构精度高，但缺乏有限样本可靠性保证，这在以插补值指导调度和规划的电力系统中是关键缺陷。

Method: SPLICE框架包含四个关键模块：（1）JEPA编码器将日负荷段映射到64维潜空间；（2）条件潜桥（conditional latent bridge）通过四种采样模式生成候选间隙轨迹；（3）逐时解码器将潜空间映射回信号空间；（4）自适应共形推断（ACI）为输出提供有覆盖保证的预测区间。其中流匹配变体在5-10个ODE步长内实现与DDIM相当的质量（5-10倍加速）。

Result: 在十三个负荷数据集（九个专有、三个UCI Electricity、ETTh1）上，SPLICE平均Load-only MSE最低（0.056），在91天空隙下9/12非退化数据集最优，全空隙长度下18/32最优；CRPS最佳（0.161，比最强竞品低18.3%）；ACI实现93-95%经验覆盖，纠正静态共形预测高达7.5个百分点的欠覆盖；池化JEPA编码器可迁移至四个未见领域。

Conclusion: SPLICE通过潜变量生成式插补与分布自由的自适应共形预测的模块化组合，在保证高重构精度的同时提供了统计意义上的可靠性保证，并具备良好的计算效率（流匹配加速）和跨域迁移能力。

Abstract: Generative models for time-series imputation achieve strong reconstruction accuracy, yet provide no finite-sample reliability guarantees, a critical limitation in power systems where imputed values inform dispatch and planning. We introduce SPLICE (Self-supervised Predictive Latent Inpainting with Conformal Envelopes), a modular framework coupling latent generative imputation with distribution-free, online-adaptive prediction intervals. A JEPA encoder maps daily load segments into a 64-dimensional latent space; a conditional latent bridge with four sampling modes generates candidate gap trajectories; an hourly-conditioned decoder maps back to signal space; and Adaptive Conformal Inference (ACI) wraps the output with coverage-guaranteed prediction bands. The flow-matching variant achieves comparable quality to DDIM in 5--10 ODE steps (5-10x speedup). On thirteen load datasets (nine proprietary, three UCI Electricity, ETTh1), SPLICE achieves the lowest mean Load-only MSE (0.056), winning 9/12 non-degenerate datasets at 91-day gaps and 18/32 across all gap lengths vs. five established baselines, and produces the best CRPS (0.161, -18.3% vs. the strongest competitor). ACI delivers 93--95% empirical coverage, correcting under-coverage failures of up to 7.5 pp observed with static conformal prediction. A pooled JEPA encoder trained on nine feeds transfers to four unseen domains, matching or exceeding per-dataset oracles with only a quick bridge fine-tuning.

</details>


### [104] [Learning Fingerprints for Medical Time Series with Redundancy-Constrained Information Maximization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00130)
*Huayu Li,ZhengXiao He,Xiwen Chen,Jingjing Wang,Siyuan Tian,Jinghao Wen,Ao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Fingerprint Tokens框架，通过交叉注意力瓶颈将变长医学时间序列压缩为k个固定大小的潜在标记，结合重构损失和基于总编码率(TCR)的多样性惩罚进行训练，实现解耦且可解释的表征学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督方法（如MAE）虽能预训练通用编码器，但无法显式学习紧凑且语义可解释的潜在表征，通常依赖全局平均池化或[CLS]标记等启发式聚合策略。

Method: 提出Fingerprint Tokens框架：使用交叉注意力瓶颈生成固定数量k个潜在标记；设计双重目标函数——重构损失确保标记是原始数据的充分统计量，基于总编码率(TCR)的多样性惩罚最小化标记间冗余；将其理论化为解耦率失真问题。

Result: 产生低维、可解释、样本高效的表示，每个标记被鼓励捕获独立的变异因子，为更鲁棒的数字化生物标志物铺平道路。

Conclusion: 该方法通过解耦率失真框架，在压缩和可解释性之间取得平衡，有望推动医学时间序列分析中更可靠的数字生物标志物发现。

Abstract: Learning meaningful representations from medical time series (MedTS) such as ECG or EEG signals is a critical challenge. These signals are often high-dimensional, variable-length and rife with noise. Existing self-supervised approaches, such as Masked Autoencoders (MAEs) are highly effective for pre-training general-purpose encoders. However, they do not explicitly learn compact and semantically interpretable latent representations, typically relying on heuristic aggregation strategies such as global average pooling or a designated [CLS] token. We propose a novel framework that compresses a variable-length MedTS into a fixed-size set of $k$ latent Fingerprint Tokens. Our architecture employs a cross-attention bottleneck to generate these tokens and is trained with a dual-objective function. The first objective is a reconstruction loss, which ensures the tokens are \textit{sufficient statistics} for the original data. The second, a diversity penalty based on the Total Coding Rate (TCR), explicitly minimizes the redundancy between tokens, encouraging them to become statistically \textit{disentangled} representations. We present the theoretical justification for our method, framing it as a novel \textbf{Disentangled Rate-Distortion} problem. This approach produces a low-dimensional, interpretable, and sample-efficient representation, where each token is encouraged to capture an independent factor of variation, paving the way for more robust digital biomarkers.

</details>


### [105] [Smart Profit-Aware Crop Advisory System: Kisan AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00133)
*Debasis Dwibedy,Avyay Nishtala,Pranathi Mukku,D Snehaja*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文开发了Kisan AI，一个将市场价格纳入作物推荐系统的智能平台。通过Random Forest模型（准确率99.3%）结合九维特征（含市场价），并集成了价格预测、病害检测和多语言AI聊天机器人功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有作物咨询系统存在"经济盲目性"（economic blindness），即仅优化生物产量而忽视市场价格，导致农民做出农学上合理但经济上不可行的决策。

Method: 1) 在标准7个农学特征基础上增加market_price变量构建九特征数据集；2) 训练Random Forest分类器并与8个基线模型对比；3) 将RF模型集成到渐进式Web应用中，配套Facebook Prophet六个月价格预测引擎、MobileNetV2病害检测模块及基于Claude API的九语言AI聊天机器人。

Result: Random Forest模型在九特征数据集上达到99.3%的最高准确率和最低Log Loss，验证了纳入市场价格作为预测特征的有效性。

Conclusion: Kisan AI通过多模块集成的移动端平台，为印度农民提供了利润感知的作物推荐解决方案，弥合了农学优化与经济可行性之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Modern crop advisory systems exhibit a critical limitation termed \textit{economic blindness}. These systems primarily optimize for biological yield, often overlooking market price, which can lead farmers toward agronomically sound yet financially unviable decisions. In this paper, we develop Kisan AI, a smart profit-aware crop advisory system that resolves the above-mentioned limitation through a research-driven, full-stack application. We train the Random Forest(RF) classifier model on a nine-feature benchmark dataset, the standard seven agronomic attributes augmented with a \textit{market\_price} variable, and evaluated against eight baseline models, considering the evaluation matrices, such as, accuracy, precision, recall, F1-score, and Log Loss. The RF model achieves the highest accuracy of 99.3\% and the lowest Log Loss, confirming that the inclusion of market price as a predictive feature is both valid and impactful. We then implement the RF model within a multilingual progressive Web App alongside a Facebook Prophet six-month price forecasting engine and a MobileNetV2 disease detection module. A nine-language AI chatbot powered by the Anthropic Claude API unifies all modules into a single, mobile-installable platform accessible to farmers across India.

</details>


### [106] [Hierarchical Abstract Tree for Cross-Document Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00529)
*Ziwen Zhao,Menglin Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Ψ-RAG 提出了一种基于迭代“合并-折叠”过程的层次化抽象树索引构建方法，结合多粒度检索智能体，有效解决了跨文档多跳问答中现有 Tree-RAG 方法的分布适应性差、结构隔离和粗粒度抽象问题，在基准测试中平均 F1 分数分别比 RAPTOR 和 HippoRAG 2 提升 25.9% 和 7.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Tree-RAG 方法在扩展到跨文档多跳问题时面临三个关键挑战：(1) 分布适应性差——k-means 聚类因刚性分布假设引入噪声；(2) 结构隔离——树索引缺少显式的跨文档连接；(3) 粗粒度抽象——丢失了细粒度的细节信息。

Method: Ψ-RAG 包含两个核心组件：(1) 层次化抽象树索引——通过迭代的“合并-折叠”（merging and collapse）过程构建，无需先验分布假设即可自适应数据分布；(2) 多粒度检索智能体——通过重新组织的查询和智能体驱动的混合检索器，智能地与知识库交互。

Result: 在跨文档多跳问答基准上，Ψ-RAG 的平均 F1 分数比 RAPTOR 提高 25.9%，比 HippoRAG 2 提高 7.4%。支持从 token 级问答到文档级摘要的多种任务。

Conclusion: Ψ-RAG 通过自适应分布假设的树索引构建和智能体驱动的多粒度检索，有效解决了跨文档多跳问答的挑战，显著提升了检索增强生成的效果。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) enhances large language models with external knowledge, and tree-based RAG organizes documents into hierarchical indexes to support queries at multiple granularities. However, existing Tree-RAG methods designed for single-document retrieval face critical challenges in scaling to cross-document multi-hop questions: (1) poor distribution adaptability, where $k$-means clustering introduces noise due to rigid distribution assumptions; (2) structural isolation, as tree indexes lack explicit cross-document connections; and (3) coarse abstraction, which obscures fine-grained details. To address these limitations, we propose $Ψ$-RAG, a tree-RAG framework with two key components. First, a hierarchical abstract tree index built through an iterative "merging and collapse" process that adapts to data distributions without a priori assumption. Second, a multi-granular retrieval agent that intelligently interacts with the knowledge base with reorganized queries and an agent-powered hybrid retriever. $Ψ$-RAG supports diverse tasks from token-level question answering to document-level summarization. On cross-document multi-hop QA benchmarks, it outperforms RAPTOR by 25.9% and HippoRAG 2 by 7.4% in average F1 score. Code is available at https://github.com/Newiz430/Psi-RAG.

</details>


### [107] [Technical Report: Activation Residual Hessian Quantization (ARHQ) for Low-Bit LLM Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00140)
*YiFeng Wang,Zhun Sun,Keisuke Sakaguchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ARHQ是一种后训练权值分割方法，通过构建激活量化残差的Hessian矩阵来识别误差敏感权重方向，并将其分离到高精度低秩分支中，从而在低比特量化下提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特激活-权值量化中存在的误差传播问题导致模型性能严重下降，现有方法难以有效识别和隔离误差敏感的权重方向。

Method: 利用激活量化残差(G_x)构建输入侧残差Hessian矩阵，通过对缩放后的权重矩阵W G^{1/2}_x进行闭式截断SVD，将误差敏感的权重方向分离到高精度低秩分支中。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507模型上，ARHQ显著提升了逐层信噪比(SNR)，并在ZebraLogic任务上保持了推理性能，即使在激进量化条件下。

Conclusion: ARHQ通过解析性识别误差敏感方向的权值分割策略，有效缓解了低比特量化中的误差传播问题，在强量化条件下仍能保持模型推理能力。

Abstract: We present Activation Residual Hessian Quantization (ARHQ), a post-training weight splitting method designed to mitigate error propagation in low-bit activation-weight quantization. By constructing an input-side residual Hessian from activation quantization residuals (G_x), ARHQ analytically identifies and isolates error-sensitive weight directions into a high-precision low-rank branch. This is achieved via a closed-form truncated SVD on the scaled weight matrix W G^{1/2}_x . Experimental results on Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507 demonstrate that ARHQ significantly improves layer-wise SNR and preserves downstream reasoning performance on ZebraLogic even under aggressive quantization. The code is available at https://github.com/BeautMoonQ/ARHQ.

</details>


### [108] [Consistent Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00161)
*Hasan Amin,Yuan Gao,Yaser Souri,Subhojit Som,Ming Yin,Rajiv Khanna,Xia Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多路径离散一致性（MPDC）原则，并实例化为一致性扩散语言模型（CDLM），通过训练去噪器在随机后验桥上保持路径不变性，实现单阶段、无教师的高质量快速离散文本生成，在少步采样下取得最先进结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型虽有望实现亚线性时间并行生成，但高质量样本仍需数百步细化；连续域的一致性训练因依赖概率流ODE而无法直接适配离散扩散，因为离散空间不存在样本空间ODE。需要一种离散域内加速扩散的原则性方法。

Method: 提出MPDC原则，训练去噪器在精确后验桥（随机桥，而非确定性轨迹）上满足期望路径不变性；实例化为CDLM，一种单阶段、无教师蒸馏的训练框架，统一了掩码扩散、连续一致性模型和渐进/离散蒸馏。

Result: 在条件和无条件文本生成上均达到新最优，一致超越强基线离散扩散模型，甚至常超越多阶段蒸馏基线；在少步采样场景下增益最大。

Conclusion: CDLM为快速、高保真离散生成模型提供了原则性且可扩展的基础，有望成为下一代离散生成建模的基石。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) are an attractive alternative to autoregressive models because they promise sublinear-time, parallel generation, yet practical gains remain elusive as high-quality samples still demand hundreds of refinement steps. In continuous domains, consistency training along the probability-flow ODE is a popular recipe to accelerate diffusion. For discrete diffusion, no analogous sample-space ODE exists, making direct adaptation ill-defined. We argue that the natural discrete substitute is not a deterministic trajectory but its stochastic counterpart: the exact posterior bridge, available in closed form for broad corruption families including masked and uniform diffusion. Building on this observation, we introduce Multi-Path Discrete Consistency (MPDC), a new principle that trains a denoiser to be path-invariant in expectation across these stochastic bridges, and instantiate it as the Consistent Diffusion Language Model (CDLM), a single-stage, teacher-free training framework. A single CDLM objective unifies masked diffusion, continuous consistency models, and progressive/discrete distillation as analytic limits or empirical approximations of one common view. Empirically, CDLM establishes a new state of the art on both conditional and unconditional text-generation, consistently outperforming strong base discrete diffusion models and often even multi-stage distilled baselines across sampling budgets, with the largest gains in the few-step regime. Together, these results position CDLM as a principled and scalable foundation for the next generation of fast, high-fidelity discrete generative modeling.

</details>


### [109] [Towards A Generative Protein Evolution Machine with DPLM-Evo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00182)
*Xinyou Wang,Liang Hong,Jiasheng Ye,Zaixiang Zheng,Yu Li,Shujian Huang,Quanquan Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DPLM-Evo提出了一种进化离散扩散框架，通过显式建模替换、插入和删除操作来改进蛋白质语言模型，在无掩码扩散、可变长度生成和突变效应预测方面达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DPLM依赖基于掩码的吸收式扩散，这与蛋白质通过累积编辑逐步进化的生物学直觉相矛盾，缺乏对替换和插入/删除操作的显式预训练目标，限制了优化式后编辑和灵活引导生成。

Method: 提出进化离散扩散框架DPLM-Evo：(1) 将上采样长度的潜在对齐空间与可变长度观测序列空间解耦，实现可处理的indel感知生成；(2) 引入上下文进化噪声核，产生生物学信息丰富、上下文依赖的突变模式；(3) 在去噪过程中显式预测替换、插入和删除操作。

Result: 在序列理解任务上取得改进，在单序列设置下ProteinGym突变效应预测达到SOTA；支持可变长度模拟进化以及通过显式编辑轨迹对现有蛋白质进行后编辑/优化。

Conclusion: DPLM-Evo通过将离散扩散与进化生物学直觉对齐，同时提升了蛋白质序列理解和生成能力，为蛋白质工程提供了一个灵活且生物学合理的框架。

Abstract: Proteins are shaped by gradual evolution under biophysical and functional constraints. Protein language models learn rich evolutionary constraints from large-scale sequences, and discrete diffusion-based protein language models~(\eg, DPLMs) are promising for both understanding and generation. However, existing DPLMs typically rely on masking-based absorbing diffusion that contradicts a simple biological intuition: proteins evolve through accumulated edits, not by emerging from masks. Consequently, these frameworks lack explicit pretraining objectives for substitution and insertion/deletion (indel) operations, limiting both optimization-style post-editing and flexible guided generation. To address these limitations, we present DPLM-Evo, an evolutionary discrete diffusion framework that explicitly predicts substitution, insertion, and deletion operations during denoising. DPLM-Evo decouples an upsampled-length latent alignment space from the variable-length observed sequence space, which makes indel-aware generation tractable and enables adaptive scaffold growth throughout the process with negligible computational overhead. To better align substitutions with real evolution, we further introduce a contextualized evolutionary noising kernel that produces biologically informed, context-dependent mutation patterns. Across tasks, DPLM-Evo improves sequence understanding and achieves state-of-the-art mutation effect prediction performance on ProteinGym in the single-sequence setting. It also enables variable-length simulated evolution, and post-editing/optimization of existing proteins via explicit edit trajectories.

</details>


### [110] [Introducing WARM-VR: Benchmark Dataset for Multimodal Wearable Affect Recognition in Virtual Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00184)
*Karim Alghoul,Faisal Mohd,Fedwa Laamarti,Hussein Al Osman,Abdulmotaleb El Saddik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了WARM-VR，一个面向沉浸式多感官VR环境的多模态情感识别公开数据集，包含31名参与者的可穿戴传感器生理数据（BVP、EDA、皮肤温度、加速度、ECG）及视听嗅觉同步刺激，通过问卷统计验证了VR放松尤其嗅觉增强能显著降低负面情绪，并基于多种机器学习算法建立了基准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感识别数据集多聚焦于静态环境，缺乏对沉浸式多媒体场景（如VR）的适用性，无法支持在真实沉浸式多感官交互中的情感计算研究。

Method: 收集31名19-37岁参与者的可穿戴传感器数据（腕带：BVP、EDA、皮肤温度、三轴加速度；胸带：ECG），在VR中通过算术任务诱导压力后再呈现放松沙滩环境，同步整合视觉、听觉和嗅觉刺激，使用自陈问卷和生理测量进行情感评估，并采用CNN、CNN-Bi-GRU、Transformer等机器学习模型建立分类基准。

Result: 问卷分析表明VR放松显著降低了负面情绪，嗅觉增强效果更佳；BVP数据的效价二分类中CNN和CNN-Bi-GRU均达F1-score 0.63和AUC 0.69；唤醒度分类中轻量级Transformer表现最均衡（F1-0 0.54, F1-1 0.63）；放松任务中CNN-Bi-GRU取得最高性能（平均F1-score 0.64, AUC 0.69）。

Conclusion: WARM-VR数据集填补了沉浸式VR多模态情感识别领域的空白，验证了多感官刺激（尤其是嗅觉增强）对情绪调节的有效性，并提供了可靠的机器学习基准，为未来VR环境下的情感计算研究奠定基础。

Abstract: With the growing integration of human-computer interaction into everyday life, advances in machine learning have enabled systems to better perceive and respond to users' emotional states. Most existing affect recognition datasets focus on static environments, limiting their applicability to immersive multimedia contexts such as Virtual Reality (VR). In this paper, we introduce WARM-VR, a novel publicly available multimodal dataset designed to support affect recognition in immersive, multisensory environments using wearable sensing instrumentation. Data were collected from 31 participants aged 19-37 using wearable sensors: a wristband measuring Blood Volume Pulse (BVP), EDA, skin Temperature, three-axis Acceleration, and a chest strap recording ECG signals. Participants engaged in immersive VR experiences designed to elicit relaxation through a calming beach environment following stress induction via an arithmetic task. These sessions incorporated synchronized multimedia stimuli: visual, auditory, and olfactory. Affective states were assessed subjectively through validated self-report questionnaires and objectively through the analysis of physiological measurements. Statistical analysis of the questionnaires confirmed that VR relaxation significantly reduced negative affect, particularly with olfactory enhancement. Furthermore, we established a benchmark on the dataset using widely recognized machine learning algorithms. The best performance for binary classification from BVP data of valence, was obtained with a CNN and a CNN-Bi-GRU model, both achieving an average F1-score of 0.63 and an AUC of 0.69. For arousal, a lightweight Transformer architecture provided the most balanced results (F1-0 0.54 and F1-1 0.63), outperforming recurrent hybrids. In the relaxation task, a CNN-Bi-GRU model reached the highest overall performance (average F1-score 0.64, AUC 0.69).

</details>


### [111] [Fair Dataset Distillation via Cross-Group Barycenter Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00185)
*Mohammad Hossein Moslemi,Nima Hosseini Dashtbayaz,Zhimin Mei,Boyu Wang,Bissan Ghaddar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示数据集蒸馏过程中会因不同子群体预测模式差异而产生公平性差距，并提出通过蒸馏向群体不平衡无关的重心（barycenter）表示来缓解偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集蒸馏方法专注于保持整体预测性能，但忽略了不同人口统计学子群体之间的预测模式差异，导致某些子群体性能显著下降，造成公平性差距。

Method: 形式化分析了两类偏差来源（群体不平衡与预测模式差异）的相互作用，提出将蒸馏目标定位为群体不平衡无关的预测信息重心（barycenter），使所有子群体共享相似的表示，从而减少公平性问题。

Result: 该方法与现有蒸馏方法兼容，实验证明能显著降低数据集蒸馏引入的偏差。

Conclusion: 仅纠正群体不平衡无法消除公平性差距，因为根本原因在于子群体预测模式的固有差异；转向共享的聚合表示进行蒸馏可以有效缓解这一问题。

Abstract: Dataset Distillation aims to compress a large dataset into a small synthetic one while maintaining predictive performance. We show that as different demographic groups exhibit distinct predictive patterns, the distillation process struggles to simultaneously preserve informative signals for all subgroups, regardless of whether group sizes are mildly or severely imbalanced. Consequently, models trained on distilled data can experience substantial performance drops for certain subgroups, leading to fairness gaps. Crucially, these gaps do not disappear by merely correcting group imbalance, since they stem from fundamental mismatches in subgroup predictive patterns rather than from sample-size disparities alone. We therefore formally analyze the interaction between these two sources of bias and cast the solution as identifying a group-imbalance-agnostic barycenter of the predictive information that induces similar representations across all subgroups. By distilling toward this shared aggregate representation, we show that group fairness concerns can be reduced. Our approach is compatible with existing distillation methods, and empirical results show that it substantially reduces bias introduced by dataset distillation.

</details>


### [112] [Diversity in Large Language Models under Supervised Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00195)
